When Fine-Tuning Changes the Evidence: Architecture-Dependent Semantic Drift in Chest X-Ray Explanations
Authors: Kabilan Elangovan, Daniel Ting
First: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transfer learning followed by fine-tuning is widely adopted in medical image classification due to consistent gains in diagnostic performance. However, in multi-class settings with overlapping visual features, improvements in accuracy do not guarantee stability of the visual evidence used to support predictions. We define semantic drift as systematic changes in the attribution structure supporting a model's predictions between transfer learning and full fine-tuning, reflecting potential shifts in underlying visual reasoning despite stable classification performance. Using a five-class chest X-ray task, we evaluate DenseNet201, ResNet50V2, and InceptionV3 under a two-stage training protocol and quantify drift with reference-free metrics capturing spatial localization and structural consistency of attribution maps. Across architectures, coarse anatomical localization remains stable, while overlap IoU reveals pronounced architecture-dependent reorganization of evidential structure. Beyond single-method analysis, stability rankings can reverse across LayerCAM and GradCAM++ under converged predictive performance, establishing explanation stability as an interaction between architecture, optimization phase, and attribution objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：微调如何改变证据：胸部X光图像解释中的架构依赖语义漂移
迁移学习后接微调在医学图像分类中因持续提升诊断性能而被广泛采用。然而，在多类别且视觉特征重叠的场景中，准确率的提升并不保证支持预测的视觉证据结构稳定。我们定义语义漂移为模型在迁移学习与完全微调阶段支持预测的归因结构发生系统性变化，反映潜在视觉推理机制的转变，尽管分类性能稳定。在五类胸部X光任务中，我们评估DenseNet201、ResNet50V2和InceptionV3在两阶段训练协议下的表现，并使用无参考指标量化归因图的空间定位与结构一致性。不同架构下，粗略解剖定位保持稳定，但IoU重叠度揭示了架构依赖的证据结构重组。除单方法分析外，当预测性能收敛时，LayerCAM与GradCAM++的稳定性排名可能反转，表明解释稳定性是架构、优化阶段与归因目标相互作用的结果。
Summary / 总结
Transfer learning followed by fine-tuning is widely adopted in medical image classification due to consistent gains in diagnostic performance.
鉴于微调医学图像模型虽能提升诊断准确率，但未必保证视觉解释的稳定性，本研究探讨了胸部X光图像预测中架构相关的语义漂移现象。在DenseNet201、ResNet50V2和InceptionV3三种架构上，采用两阶段训练协议，作者通过无参考指标量化了从迁移学习到完全微调过程中归因图的变化。结果表明，尽管粗略解剖定位保持稳定，但IoU重叠度揭示了不同架构下证据结构的显著重组。此外，在预测性能收敛时，LayerCAM与GradCAM++的稳定性排序亦不一致，说明解释稳定性取决于模型架构、优化阶段及归因方法的交互作用。
Splits! Flexible Sociocultural Linguistic Investigation at Scale
Authors: Eylon Caplan, Tania Chakraborty, Dan Goldwasser
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-04-06T23:17:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:52:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Variation in language use, shaped by speakers' sociocultural background and specific context of use, offers a rich lens into cultural perspectives, values, and opinions. For example, Chinese students discuss "healthy eating" with words like "timing," "regularity," and "digestion," whereas Americans use vocabulary like "balancing food groups" and "avoiding fat and sugar," reflecting distinct cultural models of nutrition. The computational study of these Sociocultural Linguistic Phenomena (SLP) has traditionally been done in NLP via tailored analyses of specific groups or topics, requiring specialized data collection and experimental operationalization--a process not well-suited to quick hypothesis exploration and prototyping. To address this, we propose constructing a "sandbox" designed for systematic and flexible sociolinguistic research. Using our method, we construct a demographically/topically split Reddit dataset, Splits!, validated by self-identification and by replicating several known SLPs from existing literature. We showcase the sandbox's utility with a scalable, two-stage process that filters large collections of "potential" SLPs (PSLPs) to surface the most promising candidates for deeper, qualitative investigation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently study sociocultural linguistic variation at scale, the authors introduce Splits!, a flexible sandbox framework for systematic sociolinguistic research. They construct a Reddit dataset demographically and topically split to capture diverse language use patterns, validated through self-identification and replication of known sociolinguistic phenomena. Their two-stage method first filters large-scale potential SLPs (PSLPs) and then prioritizes the most promising candidates for qualitative analysis, demonstrating scalability and adaptability for rapid hypothesis testing without requiring specialized data collection.
为解决传统计算社会语言学研究需定制数据收集、难以快速验证假设的问题，作者提出Splits!方法，构建一个可灵活探索社会文化语言现象的沙盒。通过将Reddit数据按人口统计和主题划分，结合两阶段筛选流程，从大规模文本中识别出具有文化差异的语言模式候选项，并通过文献复现与自我标识验证其有效性。实验表明该方法可规模化发现如中西方营养话语差异等文化特异性语言现象。
What do Language Models Learn and When? The Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis
Authors: Emmy Liu, Kaiser Sun, Millicent Li, Isabelle Lee, Lindia Tjuatja, Jen-tse Huang, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-04-09T17:50:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:50:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can perform remarkably complex tasks, yet the fine-grained details of how these capabilities emerge during pretraining remain poorly understood. Scaling laws on validation loss tell us how much a model improves with additional compute, but not what skills it acquires in which order. To remedy this, we propose the Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis: pretraining follows a compositional and predictable curriculum across models and data mixtures. We test this by designing a suite of simple, composable tasks spanning retrieval, morphological transformations, coreference, logical reasoning, and mathematics. Using these tasks, we track emergence points across four model families spanning sizes from 410M-13B parameters. We find that emergence orderings of when models reach fixed accuracy thresholds are strikingly consistent ($ρ= .81$ across 45 model pairs), and that composite tasks most often emerge after their component tasks. Furthermore, we find that this structure is encoded in model representations: tasks with similar function vector representations also tend to follow similar trajectories in training. By using the space of representations derived from our task set, we can effectively predict the training trajectories of simple held-out compositional tasks throughout the course of pretraining ($R^2 = .68$-$.84$ across models) without previously evaluating them. Together, these results suggest that pretraining is more structured than loss curves reveal: skills emerge in a compositional order that is consistent across models and readable from their internals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型学习了什么以及何时学习？隐式课程假说
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够执行复杂任务，但其在预训练过程中能力如何逐步形成仍不甚明了。规模定律告诉我们模型在增加计算量时验证损失的改善程度，却无法说明模型获得技能的顺序。为解决此问题，我们提出隐式课程假说：预训练在不同模型和数据混合中遵循组合且可预测的课程。我们通过设计涵盖检索、形态变换、指代消解、逻辑推理和数学的简单可组合任务进行验证。利用这些任务，我们在410M至13B参数规模的四个模型家族中追踪能力涌现点。我们发现，模型达到固定准确率阈值的涌现顺序在45对模型中高度一致（ρ=0.81），且复合任务通常在其组成任务之后涌现。此外，我们发现该结构编码于模型表示中：功能向量表示相似的任务往往在训练中遵循相似轨迹。通过使用从任务集导出的表示空间，我们无需预先评估即可有效预测简单保留的组合任务在预训练全过程中的训练轨迹（R²=0.68-0.84）。这些结果共同表明，预训练比损失曲线揭示的结构更有序：技能以组合顺序涌现，且在不同模型间一致，可从模型内部读取。
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) can perform remarkably complex tasks, yet the fine-grained details of how these capabilities emerge during pretraining remain poorly understood.
该论文提出‘隐式课程假设’，认为大语言模型在预训练过程中以可预测的组合顺序习得能力。研究者设计了涵盖检索、形态变换、指代消解、逻辑推理和数学等任务的简单可组合任务集，并追踪不同规模模型（410M–13B参数）达到固定准确率阈值的时间点。结果发现，模型能力出现顺序高度一致（ρ=0.81），复合任务多在子任务之后出现，且任务在表示空间中的相似性与其学习轨迹高度相关。基于这些表示，研究者能有效预测未见过的组合任务的训练曲线（R²=0.68–0.84），表明预训练过程存在损失曲线无法体现的内在结构化课程。
Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents
Authors: Hang Ye, Xiaoxuan Ma, Fan Lu, Wayne Wu, Kwan-Yee Lin, Yizhou Wang
First: 2026-04-09T17:50:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:50:09+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://alvinyh.github.io/VGHuman/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Digital human generation has been studied for decades and supports a wide range of real-world applications. However, most existing systems are passively animated, relying on privileged state or scripted control, which limits scalability to novel environments. We instead ask: how can digital humans actively behave using only visual observations and specified goals in novel scenes? Achieving this would enable populating any 3D environments with digital humans at scale that exhibit spontaneous, natural, goal-directed behaviors. To this end, we introduce Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents, a coupled two-layer (world-agent) paradigm that replicates humans at multiple levels: they look, perceive, reason, and behave like real people in real-world 3D scenes. The World Layer reconstructs semantically rich 3D Gaussian scenes from real-world videos via an occlusion-aware pipeline and accommodates animatable Gaussian-based human avatars. The Agent Layer transforms these avatars into autonomous humanoid agents, equipping them with first-person RGB-D perception and enabling them to perform accurate, embodied planning with spatial awareness and iterative reasoning, which is then executed at the low level as full-body actions to drive their behaviors in the scene. We further introduce a benchmark to evaluate humanoid-scene interaction in diverse reconstructed environments. Experiments show our agents achieve robust autonomous behavior, yielding higher task success rates and fewer collisions than ablations and state-of-the-art planning methods. This work enables active digital human population and advances human-centric embodied AI. Data, code, and models will be open-sourced.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, autonomous digital humans that can behave naturally in novel 3D environments without scripted control, this work introduces Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents, a two-layer system combining a World Layer that reconstructs semantically rich 3D scenes from videos and an Agent Layer that transforms animatable avatars into autonomous agents with first-person perception and embodied planning. The agents reason iteratively and execute full-body actions to achieve goals, enabling spontaneous, goal-directed behavior. Experiments on a new benchmark demonstrate superior task success and reduced collisions compared to ablated versions and state-of-the-art planning methods, validating the system’s robustness and scalability for populating diverse virtual environments.
为实现能在新环境中自主、自然交互的可规模化数字人类，该研究提出视觉引导的人形代理系统，包含重建语义丰富3D场景的世界层与将动画人形转化为自主代理的代理层。代理层赋予人形第一人称感知与具身规划能力，支持迭代推理并执行全身动作以达成目标。实验表明，该系统在新构建环境中任务成功率更高、碰撞更少，优于消融实验和现有规划方法，验证了其在虚拟环境大规模部署的鲁棒性与可行性。
Sumo: Dynamic and Generalizable Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation
Authors: John Z. Zhang, Maks Sorokin, Jan Brüdigam, Brandon Hung, Stephen Phillips, Dmitry Yershov, Farzad Niroui, Tong Zhao, Leonor Fermoselle, Xinghao Zhu, Chao Cao, Duy Ta, Tao Pang, Jiuguang Wang, Preston Culbertson, Zachary Manchester, Simon Le Cléac'h
First: 2026-04-09T17:49:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:49:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a sim-to-real approach that enables legged robots to dynamically manipulate large and heavy objects with whole-body dexterity. Our key insight is that by performing test-time steering of a pre-trained whole-body control policy with a sample-based planner, we can enable these robots to solve a variety of dynamic loco-manipulation tasks. Interestingly, we find our method generalizes to a diverse set of objects and tasks with no additional tuning or training, and can be further enhanced by flexibly adjusting the cost function at test time. We demonstrate the capabilities of our approach through a variety of challenging loco-manipulation tasks on a Spot quadruped robot in the real world, including uprighting a tire heavier than the robot's nominal lifting capacity and dragging a crowd-control barrier larger and taller than the robot itself. Additionally, we show that the same approach can be generalized to humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, such as opening a door and pushing a table, in simulation. Project code and videos are available at \href{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}.
中文标题/摘要

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for legged robots to dynamically manipulate large, heavy objects using whole-body dexterity, this paper introduces a sim-to-real method that leverages test-time steering of a pre-trained whole-body control policy via a sample-based planner. The approach enables robots to handle diverse loco-manipulation tasks without additional training or tuning, and its performance can be further improved by adjusting the cost function at test time. Experiments on a Spot quadruped robot demonstrate successful manipulation of objects heavier and larger than the robot’s nominal capacity, such as uprighting a tire and dragging a barrier, and the method also generalizes to humanoid tasks like opening a door and pushing a table in simulation.

Differentially Private Language Generation and Identification in the Limit
Authors: Anay Mehrotra, Grigoris Velegkas, Xifan Yu, Felix Zhou
First: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We initiate the study of language generation in the limit, a model recently introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan [KM24], under the constraint of differential privacy. We consider the continual release model, where a generator must eventually output a stream of valid strings while protecting the privacy of the entire input sequence. Our first main result is that for countable collections of languages, privacy comes at no qualitative cost: we provide an $\varepsilon$-differentially-private algorithm that generates in the limit from any countable collection. This stands in contrast to many learning settings where privacy renders learnability impossible. However, privacy does impose a quantitative cost: there are finite collections of size $k$ for which uniform private generation requires $Ω(k/\varepsilon)$ samples, whereas just one sample suffices non-privately. We then turn to the harder problem of language identification in the limit. Here, we show that privacy creates fundamental barriers. We prove that no $\varepsilon$-DP algorithm can identify a collection containing two languages with an infinite intersection and a finite set difference, a condition far stronger than the classical non-private characterization of identification. Next, we turn to the stochastic setting where the sample strings are sampled i.i.d. from a distribution (instead of being generated by an adversary). Here, we show that private identification is possible if and only if the collection is identifiable in the adversarial model. Together, our results establish new dimensions along which generation and identification differ and, for identification, a separation between adversarial and stochastic settings induced by privacy constraints.
Summary / 总结
We initiate the study of language generation in the limit, a model recently introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan [KM24], under the constraint of differential privacy.
为理解隐私约束下语言生成与识别的极限行为，本文研究了连续发布模型中的差分隐私算法。对于可数语言集合，隐私不损害生成的定性能力：存在ε-差分隐私算法可在极限下生成有效字符串，但需付出Ω(k/ε)样本的定量代价（对大小为k的有限集合）。在识别问题上，隐私带来根本性限制：不存在ε-DP算法能识别包含两个无限交集且差集有限的语言集合，该条件比非隐私情形更强。在随机采样设定下，私有识别成立当且仅当集合在对抗性模型下可识别，揭示了隐私约束下对抗性与随机性模型间的本质分离。
Phantom: Physics-Infused Video Generation via Joint Modeling of Visual and Latent Physical Dynamics
Authors: Ying Shen, Jerry Xiong, Tianjiao Yu, Ismini Lourentzou
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures, CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in generative video modeling, driven by large-scale datasets and powerful architectures, have yielded remarkable visual realism. However, emerging evidence suggests that simply scaling data and model size does not endow these systems with an understanding of the underlying physical laws that govern real-world dynamics. Existing approaches often fail to capture or enforce such physical consistency, resulting in unrealistic motion and dynamics. In his work, we investigate whether integrating the inference of latent physical properties directly into the video generation process can equip models with the ability to produce physically plausible videos. To this end, we propose Phantom, a Physics-Infused Video Generation model that jointly models the visual content and latent physical dynamics. Conditioned on observed video frames and inferred physical states, Phantom jointly predicts latent physical dynamics and generates future video frames. Phantom leverages a physics-aware video representation that serves as an abstract yet informaive embedding of the underlying physics, facilitating the joint prediction of physical dynamics alongside video content without requiring an explicit specification of a complex set of physical dynamics and properties. By integrating the inference of physical-aware video representation directly into the video generation process, Phantom produces video sequences that are both visually realistic and physically consistent. Quantitative and qualitative results on both standard video generation and physics-aware benchmarks demonstrate that Phantom not only outperforms existing methods in terms of adherence to physical dynamics but also delivers competitive perceptual fidelity.

标题：幻影：通过联合建模视觉与潜在物理动态实现物理增强的视频生成
近年来，得益于大规模数据集和强大架构的推动，生成式视频建模取得了显著的视觉真实感。然而，新证据表明，仅扩大数据和模型规模并不能赋予这些系统对支配现实世界动态的物理定律的理解。现有方法往往无法捕捉或强制执行物理一致性，导致运动和动态不真实。在本工作中，我们探讨将潜在物理属性的推断直接集成到视频生成过程中，是否能使模型生成物理上合理的视频。为此，我们提出了幻影（Phantom），一种物理增强的视频生成模型，联合建模视觉内容与潜在物理动态。在观察到的视频帧和推断出的物理状态条件下，幻影联合预测潜在物理动态并生成未来视频帧。幻影利用一种物理感知的视频表示，作为底层物理的抽象但信息丰富的嵌入，从而无需显式指定复杂的物理动态和属性，即可联合预测物理动态与视频内容。通过将物理感知视频表示的推断直接集成到视频生成过程中，幻影生成的视频序列既视觉真实又物理一致。在标准视频生成和物理感知基准测试上的定量与定性结果表明，幻影不仅在遵循物理动态方面优于现有方法，还实现了具有竞争力的感知保真度。

WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents
Authors: Yao Zhang, Shijie Tang, Zeyu Li, Zhen Han, Volker Tresp
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-01-29T15:39:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:11+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026. Extended version with additional experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Web agents hold great potential for automating complex computer tasks, yet their interactions involve long-horizon, sequential decision-making with irreversible actions. In such settings, outcome-based supervision is sparse and delayed, often rewarding incorrect trajectories and failing to support inference-time scaling. This motivates the use of Process Reward Models (WebPRMs) for web navigation, but existing approaches remain limited: scalar WebPRMs collapse progress into coarse, weakly grounded signals, while checklist-based WebPRMs rely on brittle template matching that fails under layout or semantic changes and often mislabels superficially correct actions as successful, providing little insight or interpretability. To address these challenges, we introduce WebArbiter, a reasoning-first, principle-inducing WebPRM that formulates reward modeling as text generation, producing structured justifications that conclude with a preference verdict and identify the action most conducive to task completion under the current context. Training follows a two-stage pipeline: reasoning distillation equips the model with coherent principle-guided reasoning, and reinforcement learning corrects teacher biases by directly aligning verdicts with correctness, enabling stronger generalization. To support systematic evaluation, we release WebPRMBench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning four diverse web environments with rich tasks and high-quality preference annotations. On WebPRMBench, WebArbiter-7B outperforms the strongest baseline, GPT-5, by 9.1 points. In reward-guided trajectory search on WebArena-Lite, it surpasses the best prior WebPRM by up to 6.4 points, underscoring its robustness and practical value in complex web tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebArbiter：一种面向网络代理的基于原则引导推理过程的奖励模型
网络代理在自动化复杂计算机任务方面潜力巨大，但其交互涉及长时序、顺序决策与不可逆操作。此类场景下，基于结果的监督稀疏且延迟，常奖励错误轨迹，难以支持推理时扩展。这促使我们为网络导航引入过程奖励模型（WebPRM），但现有方法仍受限：标量WebPRM将进展压缩为粗略、弱关联信号；基于清单的WebPRM依赖脆弱模板匹配，易受布局或语义变化影响，常误将表面正确动作标记为成功，缺乏洞察力与可解释性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出WebArbiter，一种以推理优先、诱导原则的WebPRM，将奖励建模视为文本生成，输出结构化论证，最终给出偏好判决并识别当前情境下最有利于任务完成的动作。训练采用两阶段流程：推理蒸馏赋予模型连贯的原则引导推理能力；强化学习通过直接对齐判决与正确性修正教师偏差，实现更强泛化能力。为支持系统性评估，我们发布WebPRMBench，涵盖四个多样网络环境、丰富任务及高质量偏好标注。在WebPRMBench上，WebArbiter-7B优于最强基线GPT-5达9.1分；在WebArena-Lite的奖励引导轨迹搜索中，其超越最佳前序WebPRM最高达6.4分，凸显其在复杂网络任务中的鲁棒性与实用价值。

Quantifying Explanation Consistency: The C-Score Metric for CAM-Based Explainability in Medical Image Classification
Authors: Kabilan Elangovan, Daniel Ting
First: 2026-04-09T17:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:47:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Class Activation Mapping (CAM) methods are widely used to generate visual explanations for deep learning classifiers in medical imaging. However, existing evaluation frameworks assess whether explanations are correct, measured by localisation fidelity against radiologist annotations, rather than whether they are consistent: whether the model applies the same spatial reasoning strategy across different patients with the same pathology. We propose the C-Score (Consistency Score), a confidence-weighted, annotation-free metric that quantifies intra-class explanation reproducibility via intensity-emphasised pairwise soft IoU across correctly classified instances. We evaluate six CAM techniques: GradCAM, GradCAM++, LayerCAM, EigenCAM, ScoreCAM, and MS GradCAM++ across three CNN architectures (DenseNet201, InceptionV3, ResNet50V2) over thirty training epochs on the Kermany chest X-ray dataset, covering transfer learning and fine-tuning phases. We identify three distinct mechanisms of AUC-consistency dissociation, invisible to standard classification metrics: threshold-mediated gold list collapse, technique-specific attribution collapse at peak AUC, and class-level consistency masking in global aggregation. C-Score provides an early warning signal of impending model instability. ScoreCAM deterioration on ResNet50V2 is detectable one full checkpoint before catastrophic AUC collapse and yields architecture-specific clinical deployment recommendations grounded in explanation quality rather than predictive ranking alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化解释一致性：用于医学图像分类的基于CAM的可解释性C分数度量
类激活映射（CAM）方法广泛用于生成深度学习分类器在医学影像中的视觉解释。然而，现有评估框架仅评估解释是否正确（通过与放射科医生标注的定位保真度衡量），而非评估解释是否一致：即模型是否对具有相同病理的患者采用相同的空域推理策略。我们提出C分数（一致性分数），这是一种基于置信度加权、无需标注的度量方法，通过在正确分类样本中强调强度的成对软IoU，量化同类解释的可重复性。我们在Kermany胸部X光数据集上，对六种CAM技术（GradCAM、GradCAM++、LayerCAM、EigenCAM、ScoreCAM、MS GradCAM++）在三种CNN架构（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ResNet50V2）上进行三十个训练周期的评估，涵盖迁移学习和微调阶段。我们识别出三种标准分类指标无法检测到的AUC一致性解耦机制：阈值介导的黄金列表崩溃、技术特异性归因在AUC峰值时的崩溃，以及全局聚合中类级一致性被掩盖。C分数可作为模型即将不稳定时的早期预警信号。在ResNet50V2架构上，ScoreCAM的性能退化可在灾难性AUC崩溃前一个完整检查点被检测到，并据此提供基于解释质量而非仅预测排序的架构特定临床部署建议。

sciwrite-lint: Verification Infrastructure for the Age of Science Vibe-Writing
Authors: Sergey V Samsonau
First: 2026-04-09T17:46:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:46:44+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/authentic-research-partners/sciwrite-lint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Science currently offers two options for quality assurance, both inadequate. Journal gatekeeping claims to verify both integrity and contribution, but actually measures prestige: peer review is slow, biased, and misses fabricated citations even at top venues. Open science provides no quality assurance at all: the only filter between AI-generated text and the public record is the author's integrity. AI-assisted writing makes both worse by producing more papers faster than either system can absorb. We propose a third option: measure the paper itself. sciwrite-lint (pip install sciwrite-lint) is an open-source linter for scientific manuscripts that runs entirely on the researcher's machine (free public databases, a single consumer GPU, and open-weights models) with no manuscripts sent to external services. The pipeline verifies that references exist, checks retraction status, compares metadata against canonical records, downloads and parses cited papers, verifies that they support the claims made about them, and follows one level further to check cited papers' own bibliographies. Each reference receives a per-reference reliability score aggregating all verification signals. We evaluate the pipeline on 30 unseen papers from arXiv and bioRxiv with error injection and LLM-adjudicated false positive analysis. As an experimental extension, we propose SciLint Score, combining integrity verification with a contribution component that operationalizes five frameworks from philosophy of science (Popper, Lakatos, Kitcher, Laudan, Mayo) into computable structural properties of scientific arguments. The integrity component is the core of the tool and is evaluated in this paper; the contribution component is released as experimental code for community development.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inadequacy of current scientific quality assurance systems—journal peer review that prioritizes prestige over substance, and open science that lacks any verification—this work introduces sciwrite-lint, an open-source linter that autonomously verifies scientific manuscripts on the researcher’s local machine using public databases and open models. The tool checks reference existence, retraction status, metadata accuracy, citation support, and even recursively validates cited works’ bibliographies, assigning each reference a reliability score. Evaluated on 30 unseen arXiv and bioRxiv papers with error injection and LLM-based false positive analysis, it demonstrated high detection capability for fabricated or unsupported citations without external dependencies.
鉴于当前科学质量保障体系的不足——期刊同行评审重声望轻实质，开放科学则完全缺乏验证机制——本文提出sciwrite-lint，一个可在研究者本地机器上运行的开源校验工具，利用公开数据库和开源模型自动验证论文。该工具检查引用是否存在、是否被撤稿、元数据是否准确、引用是否支持论点，并递归验证被引论文的参考文献，为每个引用分配可靠性评分。在30篇未见过的论文上进行测试（含错误注入和LLM判定的假阳性分析），结果显示其能有效识别伪造或不支持的引用，无需依赖外部服务。
Constrained Policy Optimization with Cantelli-Bounded Value-at-Risk
Authors: Rohan Tangri, Jan-Peter Calliess
First: 2026-01-30T13:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:45:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Value-at-Risk Constrained Policy Optimization algorithm (VaR-CPO), a sample efficient and conservative method designed to optimize Value-at-Risk (VaR) constrained reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Empirically, we demonstrate that VaR-CPO is capable of safe exploration, achieving zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, a critical property that baseline methods fail to uphold. To overcome the inherent non-differentiability of the VaR constraint, we employ Cantelli's inequality to obtain a tractable approximation based on the first two moments of the cost return. Additionally, by extending the trust-region framework of the Constrained Policy Optimization (CPO) method, we provide worst-case bounds for both policy improvement and constraint violation during the training process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Cantelli界值风险约束的策略优化
我们提出了值风险约束策略优化算法（VaR-CPO），这是一种样本效率高且保守的方法，旨在优化值风险（VaR）约束的强化学习问题。实证表明，VaR-CPO能够实现安全探索，在可行环境中训练期间零约束违反，这是基线方法无法实现的关键特性。为克服VaR约束的固有不可微性，我们采用Cantelli不等式，基于成本回报的一阶和二阶矩获得可处理的近似解。此外，通过扩展约束策略优化（CPO）方法的信任域框架，我们为训练过程中的策略改进和约束违反提供了最坏情况的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and sample-efficient reinforcement learning under Value-at-Risk constraints, this paper proposes VaR-CPO, a method that uses Cantelli’s inequality to approximate the non-differentiable VaR constraint via first and second moments of cost returns. By integrating this approximation into a trust-region framework, VaR-CPO ensures worst-case bounds on both policy improvement and constraint violation during training. Empirical results show that VaR-CPO achieves zero constraint violations in feasible environments, outperforming baseline methods that frequently violate constraints despite similar performance.
为实现受VaR约束下的安全且样本高效的强化学习，本文提出VaR-CPO算法，利用Cantelli不等式将不可微的VaR约束转化为基于成本回报一阶与二阶矩的可处理近似。该方法在信任区域框架下提供策略改进与约束违反的最坏情况界。实验表明，VaR-CPO在可行环境中训练期间零次违反约束，优于常出现约束违规的基线方法。
Novel View Synthesis as Video Completion
Authors: Qi Wu, Khiem Vuong, Minsik Jeon, Srinivasa Narasimhan, Deva Ramanan
First: 2026-04-09T17:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:44:18+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://frame-crafter.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We tackle the problem of sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) using video diffusion models; given $K$ ($\approx 5$) multi-view images of a scene and their camera poses, we predict the view from a target camera pose. Many prior approaches leverage generative image priors encoded via diffusion models. However, models trained on single images lack multi-view knowledge. We instead argue that video models already contain implicit multi-view knowledge and so should be easier to adapt for NVS. Our key insight is to formulate sparse NVS as a low frame-rate video completion task. However, one challenge is that sparse NVS is defined over an unordered set of inputs, often too sparse to admit a meaningful order, so the models should be $\textit{invariant}$ to permutations of that input set. To this end, we present FrameCrafter, which adapts video models (naturally trained with coherent frame orderings) to permutation-invariant NVS through several architectural modifications, including per-frame latent encodings and removal of temporal positional embeddings. Our results suggest that video models can be easily trained to "forget" about time with minimal supervision, producing competitive performance on sparse-view NVS benchmarks. Project page: https://frame-crafter.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：将新颖视角合成视为视频补全
我们使用视频扩散模型解决稀疏新颖视角合成（NVS）问题：给定场景的K（约5）个多视角图像及其相机姿态，预测目标相机姿态下的视角。许多现有方法利用通过扩散模型编码的生成图像先验。然而，仅在单张图像上训练的模型缺乏多视角知识。我们主张视频模型已隐含多视角知识，因此更适合用于NVS。我们的核心见解是将稀疏NVS建模为低帧率视频补全任务。但一个挑战是，稀疏NVS定义在无序输入集合上，通常过于稀疏而无法赋予有意义的顺序，因此模型应对此输入集合的排列保持不变。为此，我们提出了FrameCrafter，通过若干架构修改（包括每帧潜在编码和移除时间位置嵌入），将天然训练于有序帧的视频模型适配为排列不变的NVS模型。实验结果表明，视频模型在极小监督下即可轻松训练为“遗忘”时间信息，且在稀疏视角NVS基准测试中表现优异。项目页面：https://frame-crafter.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve sparse novel view synthesis using video diffusion models, the authors propose treating the task as a low-frame-rate video completion problem, leveraging the implicit multi-view knowledge inherent in video models. They introduce FrameCrafter, an architecture that adapts video models to be permutation-invariant over unordered input views by modifying latent encodings and removing temporal positional embeddings. Experimental results show that this approach enables video models to effectively generalize to sparse-view synthesis with minimal supervision, achieving competitive performance on standard benchmarks.
为提升稀疏新视角合成性能，作者提出将该任务视为低帧率视频补全问题，利用视频模型中固有的多视角隐含知识。他们设计了FrameCrafter架构，通过修改帧级潜在编码并移除时间位置嵌入，使视频模型对输入视角的顺序不敏感。实验表明，该方法能在极小监督下使视频模型有效适应稀疏视角合成，达到主流基准的竞争力水平。
SealQA: Raising the Bar for Reasoning in Search-Augmented Language Models
Authors: Thinh Pham, Nguyen Nguyen, Pratibha Zunjare, Weiyuan Chen, Yu-Min Tseng, Tu Vu
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-06-01T16:04:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:44:11+00:00
Comments: Camera Ready version for ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce SealQA, a new challenge benchmark for evaluating SEarch-Augmented Language models on fact-seeking questions where web search yields conflicting, noisy, or unhelpful results. SealQA comes in three flavors: (1) Seal-0 (main) and (2) Seal-Hard, which assess factual accuracy and reasoning capabilities, with Seal-0 focusing on the most challenging questions where chat models (e.g., GPT-4.1) typically achieve near-zero accuracy; and (3) LongSeal, which extends SealQA to test long-context, multi-document reasoning in "needle-in-a-haystack" settings. Our evaluation reveals critical limitations in current models: Even frontier LLMs perform poorly across all SealQA flavors. On Seal-0, frontier agentic models equipped with tools like o3 and o4-mini achieve only 17.1% and 6.3% accuracy, respectively, at their best reasoning efforts. We find that advanced reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-671B and o3-mini are highly vulnerable to noisy search results. Notably, increasing test-time compute does not yield reliable gains across o3-mini, o4-mini, and o3, with performance often plateauing or even declining early. Additionally, while recent models are less affected by the "lost-in-the-middle" issue, they still fail to reliably identify relevant documents in LongSeal when faced with numerous distractors. To facilitate future work, we release SealQA at huggingface.co/datasets/vtllms/sealqa.

标题：SealQA：提升搜索增强语言模型推理能力的新基准
我们提出了SealQA，一个用于评估在搜索结果冲突、嘈杂或无用的情况下，搜索增强语言模型对事实性问题推理能力的新基准。SealQA包含三种版本：(1) Seal-0（主版本）和(2) Seal-Hard，分别评估事实准确性与推理能力，其中Seal-0聚焦于聊天模型（如GPT-4.1）通常准确率接近零的最具挑战性问题；(3) LongSeal扩展了SealQA，用于测试在“大海捞针”场景下长上下文、多文档推理能力。我们的评估揭示了当前模型的关键局限：即使是前沿的大语言模型在所有SealQA版本中表现不佳。在Seal-0上，配备o3和o4-mini等工具的前沿代理模型，即使在最佳推理努力下，准确率也分别仅为17.1%和6.3%。我们发现，如DeepSeek-R1-671B和o3-mini等先进推理模型对嘈杂搜索结果高度敏感。值得注意的是，增加测试时计算量并未在o3-mini、o4-mini和o3上带来可靠提升，性能常在早期就趋于平缓甚至下降。此外，尽管近期模型对“迷失中间”问题影响较小，但在LongSeal中面对大量干扰项时，仍无法可靠识别相关文档。为促进后续研究，我们已在huggingface.co/datasets/vtllms/sealqa发布SealQA。

Motivated by the need to evaluate how well language models reason under real-world search conditions filled with conflicting or noisy results, the authors introduce SealQA, a benchmark with three variants: Seal-0 (challenging fact-seeking questions), Seal-Hard (reasoning-focused), and LongSeal (multi-document, long-context). They find that even frontier models like o3 and o4-mini achieve only 17.1% and 6.3% accuracy on Seal-0, respectively, and that advanced models such as DeepSeek-R1-671B are highly sensitive to noisy search results. Increasing compute at test time does not reliably improve performance, and models still struggle to identify relevant documents in LongSeal despite distractors.
为评估语言模型在现实搜索环境下（常出现冲突或噪声结果）的推理能力，作者提出了SealQA基准，包含三个变体：Seal-0（事实难度高）、Seal-Hard（侧重推理）和LongSeal（多文档、长上下文）。评估结果显示，即使是最先进的模型如o3和o4-mini，在Seal-0上最高仅分别达到17.1%和6.3%的准确率。研究发现这些模型对噪声搜索结果高度敏感，增加测试时计算量并不能稳定提升性能，常出现早期平台或下降。在LongSeal中，模型仍难以在大量干扰文档中识别相关文档，尽管对“迷失中间”问题的影响有所减轻。
The Detection-Extraction Gap: Models Know the Answer Before They Can Say It
Authors: Hanyang Wang, Mingxuan Zhu
First: 2026-04-08T02:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:43:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern reasoning models continue generating long after the answer is already determined. Across five model configurations, two families, and three benchmarks, we find that 52--88% of chain-of-thought tokens are produced after the answer is recoverable from a partial prefix. This post-commitment generation reveals a structural phenomenon: the detection-extraction gap. Free continuations from early prefixes recover the correct answer even at 10% of the trace, while forced extraction fails on 42% of these cases. The answer is recoverable from the model state, yet prompt-conditioned decoding fails to extract it. We formalize this mismatch via a total-variation bound between free and forced continuation distributions, yielding quantitative estimates of suffix-induced shift. Exploiting this asymmetry, we propose Black-box Adaptive Early Exit (BAEE), which uses free continuations for both detection and extraction, truncating 70--78% of serial generation while improving accuracy by 1--5pp across all models. For thinking-mode models, early exit prevents post-commitment overwriting, yielding gains of up to 5.8pp; a cost-optimized variant achieves 68--73% reduction at a median of 9 API calls. Code is available at https://github.com/EdWangLoDaSc/know2say.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检测-提取差距：模型在能说出答案前已知答案
现代推理模型在答案已确定后仍持续生成。在五种模型配置、两个家族和三个基准测试中，我们发现52%至88%的思维链令牌是在从部分前缀可恢复答案后生成的。这种承诺后的生成揭示了一种结构性现象：检测-提取差距。早期前缀的自由延续即使在轨迹10%处即可恢复正确答案，而强制提取在42%的情况下失败。答案已从模型状态中可恢复，但提示条件下的解码无法提取它。我们通过自由延续与强制延续分布之间的总变差界形式化这一不匹配，从而量化后缀诱导的偏移。利用这一不对称性，我们提出黑盒自适应早期退出（BAEE），利用自由延续进行检测和提取，截断70%至78%的串行生成，同时在所有模型上提升准确率1至5个百分点。对于思考模式模型，早期退出可防止承诺后覆盖，最高提升5.8个百分点；一个成本优化版本在中位数9次API调用下实现68%至73%的减少。代码见https://github.com/EdWangLoDaSc/know2say。
Summary / 总结
Modern reasoning models continue generating long after the answer is already determined.
该论文研究了现代推理模型在答案已确定后仍继续生成冗余内容的原因，发现检测与提取阶段存在结构性差距。作者在五个模型配置和三个基准测试中发现，52%至88%的思维链token在答案可从部分前缀恢复后仍被生成。他们通过自由与强制延续分布之间的总变差界量化这一现象，揭示了后缀诱导的显著偏移。利用这一不对称性，提出黑盒自适应早期退出（BAEE）方法，利用自由延续进行检测与提取，实现高达78%的生成截断，同时在所有模型上提升准确率1–5个百分点，对思维模式模型最高提升5.8个百分点；优化成本版本可将API调用减少68–73%。
BoBa: Boosting Backdoor Detection through Data Distribution Inference in Federated Learning
Authors: Zhengyuan Jiang, Xingyu Lyu, Shanghao Shi, Yang Xiao, Yimin Chen, Y. Thomas Hou, Wenjing Lou, Ning Wanga
Venue: ECAI 2025
First: 2024-07-12T19:38:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:42:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning, while being a promising approach for collaborative model training, is susceptible to backdoor attacks due to its decentralized nature. Backdoor attacks have shown remarkable stealthiness, as they compromise model predictions only when inputs contain specific triggers. As a countermeasure, anomaly detection is widely used to filter out backdoor attacks in FL. However, the non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data distribution nature of FL clients presents substantial challenges in backdoor attack detection, as the data variety introduces variance among benign models, making them indistinguishable from malicious ones. In this work, we propose a novel distribution-aware backdoor detection mechanism, BoBa, to address this problem. To differentiate outliers arising from data variety versus backdoor attacks, we propose to break down the problem into two steps: clustering clients utilizing their data distribution, and followed by a voting-based detection. We propose a novel data distribution inference mechanism for accurate data distribution estimation. To improve detection robustness, we introduce an overlapping clustering method, where each client is associated with multiple clusters, ensuring that the trustworthiness of a model update is assessed collectively by multiple clusters rather than a single cluster. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that BoBa can reduce the attack success rate to lower than 0.001 while maintaining high main task accuracy across various attack strategies and experimental settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BoBa：通过数据分布推断提升联邦学习中的后门检测
联邦学习虽是协同模型训练的有前景方法，但因其去中心化特性易受后门攻击。后门攻击具有高度隐蔽性，仅在输入包含特定触发器时才会破坏模型预测。作为应对措施，异常检测常用于过滤联邦学习中的后门攻击。然而，联邦学习客户端数据分布的非独立同分布（非IID）特性给后门检测带来挑战，因数据多样性导致良性模型间存在差异，难以与恶意模型区分。 本工作中，我们提出一种新颖的分布感知后门检测机制BoBa，以解决此问题。为区分数据多样性引起的异常与后门攻击，我们将问题分解为两步：利用客户端数据分布进行聚类，随后进行投票式检测。我们提出一种新颖的数据分布推断机制，以实现准确的数据分布估计。为提升检测鲁棒性，我们引入重叠聚类方法，每个客户端关联多个聚类，确保模型更新的可信度由多个聚类共同评估，而非单一聚类。通过大量评估，我们证明BoBa可在多种攻击策略和实验设置下，将攻击成功率降至低于0.001，同时保持高主任务准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting backdoor attacks in federated learning where non-IID data distributions obscure malicious behavior, BoBa introduces a distribution-aware detection framework. It first infers each client’s data distribution and clusters them accordingly, then employs a voting-based mechanism across overlapping clusters to distinguish genuine data variance from backdoor-induced anomalies. Experimental results show BoBa successfully reduces attack success rates below 0.001 while preserving high main task accuracy across diverse attack types and settings.
为应对联邦学习中非独立同分布数据导致的后门攻击难以检测问题，BoBa提出一种基于数据分布感知的检测机制。该方法首先推断各客户端数据分布并进行聚类，随后通过多个重叠聚类的投票机制区分数据自然差异与后门攻击异常。实验表明，BoBa可将攻击成功率降至0.001以下，同时在多种攻击策略和设置下保持高主任务精度。
PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation
Authors: Runpeng Geng, Chenlong Yin, Yanting Wang, Ying Chen, Jinyuan Jia
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:42:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:42:45+00:00
Comments: To appear in ACL 2026. The code is available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PIArena：提示注入评估平台
提示注入攻击对各类现实应用构成严重安全风险。尽管该问题日益受到关注，但社区仍面临关键缺口：缺乏统一的提示注入评估平台。这使得可靠比较防御措施、理解其在多样化攻击下的真实鲁棒性，或评估其在不同任务和基准上的泛化能力变得困难。例如，许多最初被报告为有效的防御措施，后来被发现对多样化数据集和攻击手段的鲁棒性有限。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了PIArena，一个统一且可扩展的提示注入评估平台，使用户能够轻松集成最先进的攻击与防御方法，并在多种现有及新基准上进行评估。我们还设计了一种基于动态策略的攻击方法，可根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。通过PIArena的全面评估，我们揭示了当前最先进防御措施的关键局限：任务间泛化能力有限、易受自适应攻击影响，以及当注入任务与目标任务一致时所面临的根本性挑战。代码与数据集可在 https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena 获取。
Summary / 总结
Prompt injection attacks threaten real-world AI systems, yet the field lacks a unified evaluation platform, hindering reliable defense comparison and robustness assessment. PIArena addresses this by offering an extensible framework for testing attacks and defenses across diverse benchmarks, including a dynamic strategy-based attack that adapts prompts based on defense feedback. Experimental results reveal that current defenses often fail to generalize across tasks, are vulnerable to adaptive attacks, and struggle when injected tasks align with target tasks, exposing critical gaps in existing security measures.
提示注入攻击对现实世界AI系统构成严重威胁，但领域内缺乏统一评估平台，阻碍了防御方案的可靠比较与鲁棒性评估。PIArena通过提供可扩展框架，支持在多种基准上测试攻击与防御策略，包括一种基于防御反馈动态优化提示的攻击方法。实验结果表明，当前防御方案普遍存在任务泛化能力不足、易受自适应攻击影响，且在注入任务与目标任务一致时表现不佳，揭示了现有安全措施的关键缺陷。
Bridging the Gap between Micro-scale Traffic Simulation and 4D Digital Cityscapes
Authors: Longxiang Jiao, Lukas Hofmann, Yiru Yang, Zhanyi Wu, Jonas Egeler
First: 2026-04-09T17:40:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:40:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While micro-scale traffic simulations provide essential data for urban planning, they are rarely coupled with the high-fidelity visualization or auralization necessary for effective stakeholder communication. In this work, we present a real-time 4D visualization framework that couples the SUMO traffic with a photorealistic, geospatially accurate VR representation of Zurich in Unreal Engine 5. Our architecture implements a robust C++ data pipeline for synchronized vehicle visualization and features an Open Sound Control (OSC) interface to support external auralization engines. We validate the framework through a user study assessing the correlation between simulated traffic dynamics and human perception. Results demonstrate a high degree of perceptual alignment, where users correctly interpret safety risks from the 4D simulation. Furthermore, our findings indicate that the inclusion of spatialized audio alters the user's sense of safety, showing the importance of multimodality in traffic simulations.

Density-Driven Optimal Control: Convergence Guarantees for Stochastic LTI Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Kooktae Lee
First: 2026-04-09T17:39:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:39:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the decentralized non-uniform area coverage problem for multi-agent systems, a critical task in missions with high spatial priority and resource constraints. While existing density-based methods often rely on computationally heavy Eulerian PDE solvers or heuristic planning, we propose Stochastic Density-Driven Optimal Control (D$^2$OC). This is a rigorous Lagrangian framework that bridges the gap between individual agent dynamics and collective distribution matching. By formulating a stochastic MPC-like problem that minimizes the Wasserstein distance as a running cost, our approach ensures that the time-averaged empirical distribution converges to a non-parametric target density under stochastic LTI dynamics. A key contribution is the formal convergence guarantee established via reachability analysis, providing a bounded tracking error even in the presence of process and measurement noise. Numerical results verify that Stochastic D$^2$OC achieves robust, decentralized coverage while outperforming previous heuristic methods in optimality and consistency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：密度驱动的最优控制：随机线性时不变多智能体系统的收敛保证
本文针对多智能体系统中的分布式非均匀区域覆盖问题，该问题在高空间优先级和资源受限的任务中至关重要。虽然现有基于密度的方法常依赖计算量大的欧拉型偏微分方程求解器或启发式规划，我们提出随机密度驱动最优控制（Stochastic D²OC），这是一种严谨的拉格朗日框架，弥合了个体智能体动力学与群体分布匹配之间的差距。通过将最小化Wasserstein距离作为运行成本的随机MPC类问题，我们的方法确保在随机线性时不变动力学下，时间平均经验分布收敛至非参数目标密度。关键贡献是通过可达性分析建立形式化的收敛保证，即使在过程和测量噪声存在的情况下，仍能提供有界的跟踪误差。数值结果验证了随机D²OC在实现鲁棒分布式覆盖的同时，在最优性和一致性方面优于以往启发式方法。

This paper addresses the decentralized non-uniform area coverage problem for multi-agent systems, a critical task in missions with high spatial priority and resource constraints.

What They Saw, Not Just Where They Looked: Semantic Scanpath Similarity via VLMs and NLP metric
Authors: Mohamed Amine Kerkouri, Marouane Tliba, Bin Wang, Aladine Chetouani, Ulas Bagci, Alessandro Bruno
First: 2026-04-09T17:36:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:36:22+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ETRA 2026 GenAI workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scanpath similarity metrics are central to eye-movement research, yet existing methods predominantly evaluate spatial and temporal alignment while neglecting semantic equivalence between attended image regions. We present a semantic scanpath similarity framework that integrates vision-language models (VLMs) into eye-tracking analysis. Each fixation is encoded under controlled visual context (patch-based and marker-based strategies) and transformed into concise textual descriptions, which are aggregated into scanpath-level representations. Semantic similarity is then computed using embedding-based and lexical NLP metrics and compared against established spatial measures, including MultiMatch and DTW. Experiments on free-viewing eye-tracking data demonstrate that semantic similarity captures partially independent variance from geometric alignment, revealing cases of high content agreement despite spatial divergence. We further analyze the impact of contextual encoding on description fidelity and metric stability. Our findings suggest that multimodal foundation models enable interpretable, content-aware extensions of classical scanpath analysis, providing a complementary dimension for gaze research within the ETRA community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：他们所见，而非仅注视位置：通过视觉语言模型与自然语言处理指标计算语义扫视路径相似性
扫视路径相似性度量是眼动研究的核心，但现有方法主要评估空间与时间对齐，而忽视了被注视图像区域间的语义等价性。我们提出一种语义扫视路径相似性框架，将视觉语言模型（VLMs）整合进眼动分析。每个注视点在受控视觉上下文（基于补丁和标记策略）下编码，并转化为简洁文本描述，再聚合为扫视路径级表示。随后使用基于嵌入和词法的自然语言处理指标计算语义相似性，并与传统空间度量（如MultiMatch和DTW）进行比较。在自由观看眼动数据上的实验表明，语义相似性捕捉了部分独立于几何对齐的变异，揭示了即使空间上存在差异，内容上仍高度一致的情况。我们进一步分析了上下文编码对描述保真度和指标稳定性的影响。研究结果表明，多模态基础模型可为经典扫视路径分析提供可解释、内容感知的扩展，为ETRA社区内的注视研究提供补充维度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of traditional scanpath metrics that ignore semantic content, this study introduces a framework using vision-language models to compute semantic similarity between fixations in eye-tracking data. Fixations are encoded under controlled visual contexts and converted into textual descriptions, then aggregated into scanpath representations. Semantic similarity is measured via NLP metrics and compared with spatial alignment measures like MultiMatch and DTW. Results show that semantic similarity captures distinct variance from spatial alignment, revealing cases of high content agreement despite spatial divergence, and that context encoding significantly affects description fidelity and metric stability.
为弥补传统扫视路径度量忽略语义内容的不足，该研究提出一种结合视觉-语言模型的语义扫视路径相似性框架。通过控制视觉上下文将注视点编码并转化为文本描述，再聚合为扫视路径表示，利用NLP指标计算语义相似度，并与空间对齐度量（如MultiMatch和DTW）对比。实验表明，语义相似度能捕捉与空间对齐独立的变异，揭示了空间差异但内容高度一致的情况，且上下文编码显著影响描述准确性和指标稳定性。
The Impact of Dimensionality on the Stability of Node Embeddings
Authors: Tobias Schumacher, Simon Reichelt, Markus Strohmaier
First: 2026-04-09T17:33:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:33:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Previous work has established that neural network-based node embeddings return different outcomes when trained with identical parameters on the same dataset, just from using different training seeds. Yet, it has not been thoroughly analyzed how key hyperparameters such as embedding dimension could impact this instability. In this work, we investigate how varying the dimensionality of node embeddings influences both their stability and downstream performance. We systematically evaluate five widely used methods -- ASNE, DGI, GraphSAGE, node2vec, and VERSE -- across multiple datasets and embedding dimensions. We assess stability from both a representational perspective and a functional perspective, alongside performance evaluation. Our results show that embedding stability varies significantly with dimensionality, but we observe different patterns across the methods we consider: while some approaches, such as node2vec and ASNE, tend to become more stable with higher dimensionality, other methods do not exhibit the same trend. Moreover, we find that maximum stability does not necessarily align with optimal task performance. These findings highlight the importance of carefully selecting embedding dimension, and provide new insights into the trade-offs between stability, performance, and computational effectiveness in graph representation learning.

标题：维度对节点嵌入稳定性的影响
先前研究已证实，基于神经网络的节点嵌入在使用相同参数训练同一数据集时，仅因训练种子不同就会产生不同结果。然而，尚未深入分析关键超参数（如嵌入维度）如何影响这种不稳定性。本文研究了节点嵌入维度变化对其稳定性和下游性能的影响。我们系统评估了五种广泛使用的方法——ASNE、DGI、GraphSAGE、node2vec 和 VERSE——在多个数据集和嵌入维度上的表现。从表征和功能两个角度评估稳定性，并结合性能评估。结果表明，嵌入稳定性随维度变化显著不同，但不同方法呈现不同趋势：部分方法（如 node2vec 和 ASNE）在高维度下趋于更稳定，而其他方法则无此趋势。此外，我们发现最大稳定性并不一定对应最佳任务性能。这些发现强调了谨慎选择嵌入维度的重要性，并为图表示学习中稳定性、性能与计算效率之间的权衡提供了新见解。

Previous work has established that neural network-based node embeddings return different outcomes when trained with identical parameters on the same dataset, just from using different training seeds.
Figures as Interfaces: Toward LLM-Native Artifacts for Scientific Discovery
Authors: Yifang Wang, Rui Sheng, Erzhuo Shao, Yifan Qian, Haotian Li, Nan Cao, Dashun Wang
First: 2026-04-09T17:30:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:30:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are transforming scientific workflows, not only through their generative capabilities but also through their emerging ability to use tools, reason about data, and coordinate complex analytical tasks. Yet in most human-AI collaborations, the primary outputs, figures, are still treated as static visual summaries: once rendered, they are handled by both humans and multimodal LLMs as images to be re-interpreted from pixels or captions. The emergent capabilities of LLMs open an opportunity to fundamentally rethink this paradigm. In this paper, we introduce the concept of LLM-native figures: data-driven artifacts that are simultaneously human-legible and machine-addressable. Unlike traditional plots, each artifact embeds complete provenance: the data subset, analytical operations and code, and visualization specification used to generate it. As a result, an LLM can "see through" the figure--tracing selections back to their sources, generating code to extend analyses, and orchestrating new visualizations through natural-language instructions or direct manipulation. We implement this concept through a hybrid language-visual interface that integrates LLM agents with a bidirectional mapping between figures and underlying data. Using the science of science domain as a testbed, we demonstrate that LLM-native figures can accelerate discovery, improve reproducibility, and make reasoning transparent across agents and users. More broadly, this work establishes a general framework for embedding provenance, interactivity, and explainability into the artifacts of modern research, redefining the figure not as an end product, but as an interface for discovery. For more details, please refer to the demo video available at www.llm-native-figure.com.

Experiments in the science of science domain show that this approach accelerates discovery, enhances reproducibility, and improves reasoning transparency across human and AI collaborators, redefining figures as dynamic interfaces rather than static outputs.
为充分利用大语言模型在数据分析与协作中的能力，本文提出“LLM原生图表”概念，即嵌入完整数据溯源、可被人类阅读且可被大模型直接操作的交互式数据制品。方法通过构建语言与视觉双向映射接口，将大模型与底层数据关联，使其能追溯分析路径、生成代码或根据自然语言指令创建新可视化。在“科学的科学”领域实验表明，该方法显著加速发现过程、提升可复现性与跨用户/模型的推理透明度，将图表重新定义为动态交互界面而非静态结果。
Formalizing building-up constructions of self-dual codes through isotropic lines in Lean
Authors: Jae-Hyun Baek, Jon-Lark Kim
First: 2026-04-09T17:27:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:27:46+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The purpose of this paper is two-fold. First we show that Kim's building-up construction of binary self-dual codes is equivalent to Chinburg-Zhang's Hilbert symbol construction. Second we introduce a $q$-ary version of Chinburg-Zhang's construction in order to construct $q$-ary self-dual codes efficiently. For the latter, we study self-dual codes over split finite fields \(\F_q\) with \(q \equiv 1 \pmod{4}\) through three complementary viewpoints: the building-up construction, the binary arithmetic reduction of Chinburg--Zhang, and the hyperbolic geometry of the Euclidean plane. The condition that \(-1\) be a square is the common algebraic input linking these viewpoints: in the binary case it underlies the Lagrangian reduction picture, while in the split \(q\)-ary case it produces the isotropic line governing the correction terms in the extension formulas. As an application of our efficient form of generator matrices, we construct optimal self-dual codes from the split boxed construction, including self-dual \([6,3,4]\) and \([8,4,4]\) codes over \(\GF{5}\), MDS self-dual \([8,4,5]\) and \([10,5,6]\) codes over \(\GF{13}\), and a self-dual \([12,6,6]\) code over \(\GF{13}\). These structural statements are accompanied by a Lean~4 formalization of the algebraic core.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过Lean中的等距线形式化自对偶码的构建构造
本文目的有两个方面。首先，我们证明Kim的二元自对偶码构建构造等价于Chinburg-Zhang的Hilbert符号构造。其次，我们引入Chinburg-Zhang构造的q元版本，以便高效构造q元自对偶码。对于后者，我们从三个互补视角研究在分裂有限域\(\F_q\)（其中\(q \equiv 1 \pmod{4}\)）上的自对偶码：构建构造、Chinburg-Zhang的二进制算术约简，以及欧氏平面的双曲几何。\(-1\)为平方数的条件是连接这些视角的共同代数输入：在二元情形下，它支撑Lagrangian约简图景；在分裂q元情形下，它产生控制扩展公式修正项的等距线。作为我们高效生成矩阵形式的应用，我们从分裂盒构造中构建最优自对偶码，包括\(\GF{5}\)上的自对偶\([6,3,4]\)和\([8,4,4]\)码，\(\GF{13}\)上的MDS自对偶\([8,4,5]\)和\([10,5,6]\)码，以及\(\GF{13}\)上的自对偶\([12,6,6]\)码。这些结构陈述伴随着Lean~4对代数核心的正式化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify and generalize constructions of self-dual codes, this paper demonstrates that Kim's building-up method for binary self-dual codes is equivalent to Chinburg-Zhang's Hilbert symbol approach, and extends the latter to a q-ary setting over split finite fields where q ≡ 1 mod 4. The method leverages three complementary perspectives—building-up, binary arithmetic reduction, and hyperbolic geometry—unified by the algebraic condition that -1 is a square, which governs isotropic lines and correction terms in extensions. Experimental results show the method yields optimal self-dual codes, including [6,3,4] and [8,4,4] over GF(5), MDS codes [8,4,5] and [10,5,6] over GF(13), and a [12,6,6] code over GF(13), all constructed via efficient generator matrices formalized in Lean 4.
为统一并推广自对偶码的构造方法，本文证明了Kim的二元自对偶码构建法等价于Chinburg-Zhang的Hilbert符号构造，并将其推广至q元情形，适用于q ≡ 1 (mod 4)的分裂有限域。该方法通过三个互补视角——构建法、二元算术约简与欧氏平面的双曲几何——统一于-1为平方数这一代数条件，该条件决定等距线并控制扩展公式中的修正项。实验结果表明，该方法可高效生成最优自对偶码，包括GF(5)上的[6,3,4]与[8,4,4]码、GF(13)上的MDS码[8,4,5]与[10,5,6]，以及GF(13)上的[12,6,6]码，所有构造均通过Lean 4形式化验证。
AI generates well-liked but templatic empathic responses
Authors: Emma Gueorguieva, Hongli Zhan, Jina Suh, Javier Hernandez, Tatiana Lau, Junyi Jessy Li, Desmond C. Ong
First: 2026-04-09T17:22:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:22:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent research shows that greater numbers of people are turning to Large Language Models (LLMs) for emotional support, and that people rate LLM responses as more empathic than human-written responses. We suggest a reason for this success: LLMs have learned and consistently deploy a well-liked template for expressing empathy. We develop a taxonomy of 10 empathic language "tactics" that include validating someone's feelings and paraphrasing, and apply this taxonomy to characterize the language that people and LLMs produce when writing empathic responses. Across a set of 2 studies comparing a total of n = 3,265 AI-generated (by six models) and n = 1,290 human-written responses, we find that LLM responses are highly formulaic at a discourse functional level. We discovered a template -- a structured sequence of tactics -- that matches between 83--90% of LLM responses (and 60--83\% in a held out sample), and when those are matched, covers 81--92% of the response. By contrast, human-written responses are more diverse. We end with a discussion of implications for the future of AI-generated empathy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能生成受欢迎但模板化的共情回应
近期研究表明，越来越多的人转向大型语言模型（LLMs）寻求情感支持，且人们认为LLM的回应比人类撰写的回应更具共情性。我们提出这一成功的原因：LLMs已学习并持续使用一种广受欢迎的共情表达模板。我们构建了一个包含10种共情语言“策略”的分类体系，包括情感验证和复述等，并将该体系应用于分析人类与LLMs在撰写共情回应时所使用的语言。在两项对比研究中，总计n=3,265条AI生成（由六个模型生成）和n=1,290条人类撰写的回应，我们发现LLM回应在话语功能层面高度公式化。我们发现了一种模板——一种策略的结构化序列——该模板匹配83-90%的LLM回应（在独立样本中为60-83%），且当匹配成功时，覆盖回应的81-92%。相比之下，人类撰写的回应更具多样性。我们最后讨论了这对未来AI生成共情的启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that people increasingly turn to Large Language Models (LLMs) for emotional support and rate their empathic responses higher than human-written ones, researchers propose that LLMs rely on a widely preferred but formulaic template for empathy. Using a taxonomy of 10 empathic language tactics, they analyzed 3,265 AI-generated and 1,290 human-written responses across two studies, finding that LLMs consistently follow a structured sequence of tactics that matches 83–90% of their outputs and covers 81–92% of each response. In contrast, human responses showed greater diversity, suggesting LLMs prioritize predictable, well-liked patterns over varied expression.
鉴于人们越来越多地依赖大型语言模型（LLM）获取情感支持，并且普遍认为LLM的共情回应比人类写作更优，研究者提出LLM依赖一种广受欢迎但高度模板化的共情表达方式。通过构建包含10种共情语言策略的分类体系，研究者分析了3265条AI生成与1290条人类写作的回应，发现LLM回应高度遵循一种结构化的策略序列，匹配率高达83–90%，覆盖每个回应的81–92%；相比之下，人类回应更具多样性，表明LLM更注重模板化共情而非个性化表达。
SUPERNOVA: Eliciting General Reasoning in LLMs with Reinforcement Learning on Natural Instructions
Authors: Ashima Suvarna, Kendrick Phan, Mehrab Beikzadeh, Hritik Bansal, Saadia Gabriel
First: 2026-04-09T17:16:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:16:07+00:00
Comments: 23 Pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has significantly improved large language model (LLM) reasoning in formal domains such as mathematics and code. Despite these advancements, LLMs still struggle with general reasoning tasks requiring capabilities such as causal inference and temporal understanding. Extending RLVR to general reasoning is fundamentally constrained by the lack of high-quality, verifiable training data that spans diverse reasoning skills. To address this challenge, we propose SUPERNOVA, a data curation framework for RLVR aimed at enhancing general reasoning. Our key insight is that instruction-tuning datasets containing expert-annotated ground-truth encode rich reasoning patterns that can be systematically adapted for RLVR. To study this, we conduct 100+ controlled RL experiments to analyze how data design choices impact downstream reasoning performance. In particular, we investigate three key factors: (i) source task selection, (ii) task mixing strategies, and (iii) synthetic interventions for improving data quality. Our analysis reveals that source task selection is non-trivial and has a significant impact on downstream reasoning performance. Moreover, selecting tasks based on their performance for individual target tasks outperforms strategies based on overall average performance. Finally, models trained on SUPERNOVA outperform strong baselines (e.g., Qwen3.5) on challenging reasoning benchmarks including BBEH, Zebralogic, and MMLU-Pro. In particular, training on SUPERNOVA yields relative improvements of up to 52.8\% on BBEH across model sizes, demonstrating the effectiveness of principled data curation for RLVR. Our findings provide practical insights for curating human-annotated resources to extend RLVR to general reasoning. The code and data is available at https://github.com/asuvarna31/supernova.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SUPERNOVA：通过自然指令上的强化学习在大语言模型中激发通用推理
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）在数学和代码等正式领域的推理能力。然而，LLM在需要因果推理和时序理解等能力的通用推理任务上仍存在困难。将RLVR扩展到通用推理的根本限制在于缺乏覆盖多样推理技能的高质量、可验证训练数据。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了SUPERNOVA，一个面向RLVR的高质量数据整理框架，旨在增强通用推理能力。我们的核心见解是：包含专家标注真实答案的指令微调数据集蕴含丰富的推理模式，可系统性地适配RLVR。为此，我们进行了100多项受控强化学习实验，分析数据设计选择对下游推理性能的影响。特别地，我们研究了三个关键因素：（i）源任务选择，（ii）任务混合策略，（iii）提升数据质量的合成干预。分析表明，源任务选择非平凡且对下游推理性能有显著影响；基于单个目标任务表现选择任务优于基于整体平均表现的策略。最终，使用SUPERNOVA训练的模型在BBEH、Zebralogic和MMLU-Pro等具有挑战性的推理基准上超越了强基线（如Qwen3.5）。尤其在BBEH上，不同模型规模下相对提升高达52.8%，证明了基于原则的数据整理对RLVR的有效性。我们的发现为扩展RLVR至通用推理提供了实用指导。代码和数据可在https://github.com/asuvarna31/supernova获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) beyond formal domains to support general reasoning, the authors propose SUPERNOVA, a data curation framework that leverages expert-annotated instruction-tuning datasets to generate high-quality RLVR training data. Through over 100 controlled experiments, they evaluate how source task selection, task mixing, and synthetic interventions affect reasoning performance, finding that task selection based on individual target task performance is more effective than average-based strategies. Models trained using SUPERNOVA outperform strong baselines like Qwen3.5 on benchmarks such as BBEH, Zebralogic, and MMLU-Pro, achieving up to 52.8% relative improvement on BBEH across model sizes, demonstrating the critical role of principled data curation in enhancing general reasoning via RLVR.
为将强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）扩展至通用推理领域，作者提出SUPERNOVA数据构建框架，利用专家标注的指令微调数据系统性生成高质量RLVR训练数据。通过100多次控制实验，研究评估了源任务选择、任务混合策略和合成干预对推理性能的影响，发现基于单个目标任务表现的任务选择优于基于平均表现的策略。在BBEH、Zebralogic和MMLU-Pro等基准测试中，使用SUPERNOVA训练的模型显著优于Qwen3.5等强基线模型，部分任务在不同模型规模下提升高达52.8%，验证了精心设计数据对RLVR通用推理能力提升的关键作用。
Faithful GRPO: Improving Visual Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models via Constrained Policy Optimization
Authors: Sai Srinivas Kancheti, Aditya Kanade, Rohit Sinha, Vineeth N Balasubramanian, Tanuja Ganu
First: 2026-04-09T17:15:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:15:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal reasoning models (MRMs) trained with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) show improved accuracy on visual reasoning benchmarks. However, we observe that accuracy gains often come at the cost of reasoning quality: generated Chain-of-Thought (CoT) traces are frequently inconsistent with the final answer and poorly grounded in the visual evidence. We systematically study this phenomenon across seven challenging real-world spatial reasoning benchmarks and find that it affects contemporary MRMs such as ViGoRL-Spatial, TreeVGR as well as our own models trained with standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We characterize CoT reasoning quality along two complementary axes: "logical consistency" (does the CoT entail the final answer?) and "visual grounding" (does each reasoning step accurately describe objects, attributes, and spatial relationships in the image?). To address this, we propose Faithful GRPO (FGRPO), a variant of GRPO that enforces consistency and grounding as constraints via Lagrangian dual ascent. FGRPO incorporates batch-level consistency and grounding constraints into the advantage computation within a group, adaptively adjusting the relative importance of constraints during optimization. We evaluate FGRPO on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and 3B backbones across seven spatial datasets. Our results show that FGRPO substantially improves reasoning quality, reducing the inconsistency rate from 24.5% to 1.7% and improving visual grounding scores by +13%. It also improves final answer accuracy over simple GRPO, demonstrating that faithful reasoning enables better answers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：忠实的GRPO：通过约束策略优化提升多模态语言模型的视觉空间推理能力
通过可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练的多模态推理模型（MRMs）在视觉推理基准测试中表现出更高的准确性。然而，我们观察到，这种准确性提升往往以推理质量为代价：生成的思维链（CoT）轨迹常与最终答案不一致，且缺乏对视觉证据的充分依据。我们在七个具有挑战性的现实世界空间推理基准上系统研究了这一现象，发现它影响了当前的MRMs，如ViGoRL-Spatial、TreeVGR，以及我们自己使用标准组相对策略优化（GRPO）训练的模型。我们从两个互补维度刻画CoT推理质量：“逻辑一致性”（CoT是否蕴含最终答案？）和“视觉接地性”（每一步推理是否准确描述图像中的对象、属性和空间关系？）。为解决此问题，我们提出忠实GRPO（FGRPO），它是GRPO的一种变体，通过拉格朗日对偶上升法强制执行一致性与接地性约束。FGRPO在组内优势计算中引入批级一致性与接地性约束，并在优化过程中自适应调整约束的相对重要性。我们在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和3B架构上，于七个空间数据集上评估FGRPO。结果表明，FGRPO显著提升了推理质量，将不一致性率从24.5%降至1.7%，视觉接地性得分提升13%。同时，FGRPO在最终答案准确性上优于简单GRPO，证明忠实推理可带来更优答案。

Multimodal reasoning models (MRMs) trained with reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) show improved accuracy on visual reasoning benchmarks.

LAMP: Lift Image-Editing as General 3D Priors for Open-world Manipulation
Authors: Jingjing Wang, Zhengdong Hong, Chong Bao, Yuke Zhu, Junhan Sun, Guofeng Zhang
First: 2026-04-09T17:14:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:14:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Human-like generalization in open-world remains a fundamental challenge for robotic manipulation. Existing learning-based methods, including reinforcement learning, imitation learning, and vision-language-action-models (VLAs), often struggle with novel tasks and unseen environments. Another promising direction is to explore generalizable representations that capture fine-grained spatial and geometric relations for open-world manipulation. While large-language-model (LLMs) and vision-language-model (VLMs) provide strong semantic reasoning based on language or annotated 2D representations, their limited 3D awareness restricts their applicability to fine-grained manipulation. To address this, we propose LAMP, which lifts image-editing as 3D priors to extract inter-object 3D transformations as continuous, geometry-aware representations. Our key insight is that image-editing inherently encodes rich 2D spatial cues, and lifting these implicit cues into 3D transformations provides fine-grained and accurate guidance for open-world manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \codename delivers precise 3D transformations and achieves strong zero-shot generalization in open-world manipulation. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/LAMP/.
中文标题/摘要

在开放世界中实现类人泛化仍是机器人操作的基本挑战。现有基于学习的方法，包括强化学习、模仿学习和视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs），往往难以应对新任务和未见环境。另一个有前景的方向是探索能够捕捉细粒度空间与几何关系的可泛化表示，以支持开放世界操作。虽然大语言模型（LLMs）和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能基于语言或标注2D表示提供强大的语义推理，但其有限的3D感知能力限制了其在细粒度操作中的适用性。为解决此问题，我们提出LAMP，将图像编辑提升为3D先验，以提取物体间的3D变换作为连续、几何感知的表示。我们的核心洞察是：图像编辑天然编码丰富的2D空间线索，将其隐式线索提升为3D变换，可为开放世界操作提供细粒度且精确的引导。大量实验表明，LAMP能实现精确的3D变换，并在开放世界操作中实现强大的零样本泛化能力。项目页面：https://zju3dv.github.io/LAMP/

To enable robots to generalize manipulation skills in open-world settings, the paper proposes LAMP, which leverages image-editing as a source of 3D priors to extract continuous, geometry-aware inter-object transformations.
